+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 3 (v1.1) — per-phase child budgets. Today budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase
+// gates a GLOBAL product (childSeq >= maxChildrenPerPhase * phases.length);
+// enforce it PER phase so one runaway phase parks naming itself while others are
+// unaffected, and the SUM across phases may legitimately exceed a single budget.
+describe('loop run — per-phase child budgets (item 3)', () => {
+  // A runner that ALWAYS returns BLOCKED — the phase never passes on its own.
+  const alwaysBlocked = (io) => {
+    const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
+    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
+      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
+      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'still-not-done', logPath: opts.logPath });
+    };
+    fn.calls = calls;
+    return fn;
+  };
+
+  it('RED (item 3): a phase exhausting its OWN child budget parks naming that phase (global product NOT exhausted)', async () => {
+    // 3 phases → the GLOBAL product is 2*3 = 6, far from exhausted; gate-1 alone
+    // must park at its OWN budget of 2, before the 5-iteration cap trips.
+    const spec = loopSpec({
+      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
+      phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }, { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-2', role: 'plan-reviewer' }],
+    });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
+    const runner = alwaysBlocked(io);
+    io.superviseChild = runner;
+    io.__runner = runner;
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 4, 'exhausting a single phase’s child budget parks (exit 4), not escalates at the cap');
+    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
+    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
+    assert.match(state.parkReason ?? '', /gate-1/, 'the park reason names the phase whose budget was exhausted');
+    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 2, `only the phase's own budget (2) of children was spent; got ${runner.calls.length}`);
+  });
+
+  it('GUARD (item 3): phases each UNDER their own budget complete even when the SUM exceeds one phase budget', async () => {
+    // 3 phases, budget 2 each. Each spends 2 children (BLOCKED then APPROVED) —
+    // total 6 across phases > any single budget (2), and > a naive global 2.
+    const spec = loopSpec({
+      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
+      phases: [{ id: 'p1', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p2', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p3', role: 'planner' }],
+    });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p1: 2 children
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p2: 2 children
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p3: 2 children
+    ]);
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `no per-phase budget is exceeded, so the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
+    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 6, 'the SUM of children (6) exceeds any single phase budget (2) — no global-product regression');
+  });
+});

+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 4 (v1.1) — findings persistence. Append-only .handoff/loop/findings/
+// <gate>.ndjson, one line per BLOCKED verdict {iteration?, findings, at}; the
+// next child prompt for that gate embeds the LATEST persisted findings, and
+// escalations embed them too — so a park→resume or a crash→re-invoke re-prompts
+// with the real prior findings instead of an empty string.
+describe('loop run — findings persistence (item 4)', () => {
+  const P = loopPaths('/repo');
+  const findingsLines = (io, gate) => {
+    const raw = io.files()[`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`];
+    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
+  };
+  const seedRunningState = (io, over) => {
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(P.dir, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
+      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-item4', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 0,
+      iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
+      flavor: 'loop', ...over,
+    }, null, 2) + '\n');
+  };
+
+  it('RED (4a): a BLOCKED gate then PARK then resume carries the persisted findings into the next child prompt', async () => {
+    const marker = 'DISTINCTIVE_FINDINGS_4A_MARKER';
+    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
+    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
+    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: marker }, // records the gate findings
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // model death → single-entry chain parks
+    ]);
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 4, 'run 1 parks after recording the BLOCKED findings');
+    assert.ok(findingsLines(io, 'gate-1').some((l) => String(l.findings).includes(marker)), 'the BLOCKED findings were persisted to findings/gate-1.ndjson');
+
+    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
+    io.superviseChild = runner2;
+    io.__runner = runner2;
+    await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
+    assert.ok(runner2.calls.length >= 1, 'resume re-spawned the gate child');
+    assert.match((runner2.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the resumed child prompt embeds the persisted prior findings');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (4b loop): a re-invoke with status running + a persisted findings line embeds it in the FIRST child prompt', async () => {
+    const marker = 'PERSISTED_FINDINGS_4B_LOOP';
+    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } });
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/gate-1.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: marker, at: T0 }) + '\n');
+
+    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'a child spawned on re-invoke');
+    assert.match((io.__runner.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the first re-invoke child prompt embeds the persisted findings');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (4c loop): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the PERSISTED findings in ESCALATION.md', async () => {
+    const marker = 'PERSISTED_FINDINGS_4C_LOOP';
+    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // unused — escalates before spawn
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } }); // AT cap 1
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/gate-1.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: marker, at: T0 }) + '\n');
+
+    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
+    assert.match(loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md') ?? '', new RegExp(marker), 'ESCALATION.md embeds the persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (4d): the findings file is append-only across iterations (two BLOCKED verdicts → two ordered lines)', async () => {
+    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
+    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'first-finding-A' },
+      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'second-finding-B' },
+      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
+    ]);
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
+    const lines = findingsLines(io, 'gate-1');
+    assert.equal(lines.length, 2, 'two BLOCKED verdicts append two findings lines (append-only, never overwrite)');
+    assert.match(String(lines[0].findings), /first-finding-A/, 'the first BLOCKED findings is line 1');
+    assert.match(String(lines[1].findings), /second-finding-B/, 'the second BLOCKED findings is line 2 (order preserved)');
+  });
+});

   375	  });

   377	  it('RED (D9): a smoke-approved run that completes its FINAL phase persists raw status done', async () => {
   378	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec: finalSmokeSpec, execFile: smokeExec(), runner: undefined });

   411	
   412	// ===========================================================================
   413	// ITEM 3 (v1.1) — per-phase child budgets. Today budgets.maxChildrenPerPhase
   414	// gates a GLOBAL product (childSeq >= maxChildrenPerPhase * phases.length);
   415	// enforce it PER phase so one runaway phase parks naming itself while others are
   416	// unaffected, and the SUM across phases may legitimately exceed a single budget.
   417	describe('loop run — per-phase child budgets (item 3)', () => {
   418	  // A runner that ALWAYS returns BLOCKED — the phase never passes on its own.
   419	  const alwaysBlocked = (io) => {
   420	    const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   421	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   422	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [] });
   423	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'still-not-done', logPath: opts.logPath });
   424	    };
   425	    fn.calls = calls;
   426	    return fn;
   427	  };
   428	
   429	  it('RED (item 3): a phase exhausting its OWN child budget parks naming that phase (global product NOT exhausted)', async () => {
   430	    // 3 phases → the GLOBAL product is 2*3 = 6, far from exhausted; gate-1 alone
   431	    // must park at its OWN budget of 2, before the 5-iteration cap trips.
   432	    const spec = loopSpec({
   433	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
   434	      phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }, { id: 'plan', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'gate-2', role: 'plan-reviewer' }],
   435	    });
   436	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   437	    const runner = alwaysBlocked(io);
   438	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   439	    io.__runner = runner;
   440	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   441	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'exhausting a single phase’s child budget parks (exit 4), not escalates at the cap');
   442	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
   443	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, 'the run is parked');
   444	    assert.match(state.parkReason ?? '', /gate-1/, 'the park reason names the phase whose budget was exhausted');
   445	    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 2, `only the phase's own budget (2) of children was spent; got ${runner.calls.length}`);
   446	  });
   447	
   448	  it('GUARD (item 3): phases each UNDER their own budget complete even when the SUM exceeds one phase budget', async () => {
   449	    // 3 phases, budget 2 each. Each spends 2 children (BLOCKED then APPROVED) —
   450	    // total 6 across phases > any single budget (2), and > a naive global 2.
   451	    const spec = loopSpec({
   452	      budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 2 },
   453	      phases: [{ id: 'p1', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p2', role: 'planner' }, { id: 'p3', role: 'planner' }],
   454	    });
   455	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   456	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   457	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p1: 2 children
   458	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p2: 2 children
   459	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'x' }, { verdict: 'APPROVED' }, // p3: 2 children
   460	    ]);
   461	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   462	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   463	    assert.equal(code, 0, `no per-phase budget is exceeded, so the run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
   464	    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
   465	    assert.equal(io.__runner.calls.length, 6, 'the SUM of children (6) exceeds any single phase budget (2) — no global-product regression');
   466	  });

  1266	// ===========================================================================
  1267	// ITEM 2 (v1.1) — trunk derivation. Replace hardcoded 'main' with the repo's
  1268	// actual default branch (git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → fallback
  1269	// rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD at setup), recorded in state and reused, never
  1270	// re-derived mid-run. Pinned in a MASTER-trunk fake repo — the observable is
  1271	// "master used everywhere; one derivation", not the exact command.
  1272	
  1273	// A fake git modeling a MASTER-trunk repo: the default branch is 'master'
  1274	// (symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → origin/master; fallback rev-parse
  1275	// --abbrev-ref HEAD at the ROOT → master). It answers 'main' refs LENIENTLY
  1276	// (same sha/ranges) so a main-hardcoded impl still runs end to end — the pin's
  1277	// teeth are that NO 'main' appears in the recorded git argv. deriveThrows makes
  1278	// the default-branch derivation fail (to prove a resume reuses a stamped trunk).
  1279	// Structured argv classifiers shared by the fake's dispatch AND its call-count
  1280	// helpers, so both agree on what "a trunk-derivation probe" is (verifier iter 3):
  1281	// any arg order, plus symbolic-ref HEAD and branch --show-current variants.
  1282	function isDerivationShape(args) {
  1283	  const has = (a) => args.includes(a);
  1284	  return (
  1285	    (has('symbolic-ref') && (has('refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') || has('HEAD'))) || // (a) + (d)
  1286	    (has('rev-parse') && has('--abbrev-ref') && has('HEAD')) || // (b) any order
  1287	    (has('branch') && has('--show-current')) // (c)
  1288	  );
  1289	}
  1290	// The one legit exception: rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD (any order) in a seat cwd.
  1291	function isSeatHeadProbe(args, cwd) {
  1292	  const seat = cwd === WT_A || cwd === WT_B;
  1293	  return seat && args.includes('rev-parse') && args.includes('--abbrev-ref') && args.includes('HEAD');
  1294	}

  1144	describe('pipeline — D7: escalation persists to state.json', () => {
  1145	  it('RED (D7): hitting the enforced cap exits 3 AND persists status escalated naming the gate', async () => {
  1146	    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
  1147	    const gate = 'subtask-t1-review';
  1148	    const io = makePipeRepo({
  1149	      spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks, budgets: { iterationCap: 2, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } }),
  1150	      runner: undefined,
  1151	    });
  1152	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
  1153	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
  1154	    // Seed the gate AT the enforced cap (2) — the top-of-loop cap check fires
  1155	    // before any child spawns.
  1156	    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', iterations: { [gate]: 2 } });
  1157	    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
  1158	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a capped gate escalates (exit 3)');
  1159	    // The RAW snapshot must itself be escalated — loadLoopState replays the
  1160	    // journal over the snapshot, which could MASK a state.json left 'running'.
  1161	    const rawState = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
  1162	    assert.equal(rawState.status, 'escalated', 'the RAW state.json snapshot is escalated (not merely masked by journal replay)');
  1163	    assert.ok(rawState.escalation && rawState.escalation.gate === gate, `the raw snapshot escalation names the gate; got ${JSON.stringify(rawState.escalation)}`);

  1165	    const { state } = await loadLoopState('/repo', io);
  1166	    assert.equal(state.status, LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, 'the loaded state is escalated — a resume must not see a running run');
  1167	    assert.ok(state.escalation && state.escalation.gate === gate, `the loaded escalation names the gate; got ${JSON.stringify(state.escalation)}`);
  1168	  });
  1169	});

  1183	      runner: undefined,

    93	
    94	/**
    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}

   519	            io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: smoke gate reached — review ${p.dir}/SMOKE-REVIEW.md and approve with --approve-smoke <token>\n`);
   520	            return 0;
   521	          }
   522	        } else {
   523	          // BLOCKED (or unparseable, already coerced to BLOCKED upstream).
   524	          findings = String(result.findings ?? '');
   525	          const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate: phase.id, verdict });
   526	          if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) {
   527	            atomicWriteText(
   528	              io.fs,
   529	              `${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md`,
   530	              `# Loop escalation\n\nGate '${phase.id}' exhausted its ${cap}-iteration cap on run ${runId}.\nLast findings:\n\n${findings}\n`,
   531	            );
   532	            io.stderr.write(`baton loop run: gate '${phase.id}' escalated at the cap — see ${p.dir}/ESCALATION.md\n`);
   533	            return EXIT_ESCALATED;
   534	          }
   535	        }
   536	      }
   537	    }
   538	
   539	    if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.DONE || state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount) {
   540	      io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: done — every phase completed (run ${runId})\n`);
   541	      return 0;
   542	    }
   543	    io.stdout.write(`baton loop run: stopped in status ${state.status}\n`);
   544	    return state.status === LOOP_STATUS.PARKED ? EXIT_PARKED : state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED ? EXIT_ESCALATED : 0;

   466	      if (writerCls !== 'ok') {

   479	          role: `worker-${seat}`,
   480	          assignment: writerAsg,
   481	          transcript: writerLog,
   482	          exitCode: writerResult.exitCode ?? 1,
   483	          sessionHint: state.runId,
   484	          probes: null,
   485	          avoid: [],
   486	          avoidEntries: subtaskAvoidEntries,
   487	          attempt: failoverAttempt,
   488	          loopState: { runId: state.runId, phaseIndex: state.phaseIndex, iterations: state.iterations, status: state.status },
   489	        });
   490	        if (decision.action === 'park') return park(`writer failover parked subtask '${st.id}': ${decision.reason}`);
   491	        if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   492	          // Carry entry-level avoidance across relaunches — a rejected model
   493	          // stays rejected for this subtask (H2).
   494	          if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) subtaskAvoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   495	          forcedWriter = { ...decision.assignment, role: `worker-${seat}` };
   496	        }
   497	        continue; // relaunch or retry the writer — never review a failed attempt
   498	      }
   499	      if (writerResult.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && writerResult.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   500	        findings = String(writerResult.findings ?? '');
   501	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   502	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED || (after.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   503	          return escalate(gate, findings, cap);
   504	        }
   505	        continue; // the writer retries; no reviewer sees a failed attempt
   506	      }
   507	
   508	      const post = await postflightWorktree(root, { seat, branch, mainSha, trunk }, io);
   509	      if (post.ok !== true) return park(`postflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${post.refusal}`);
   510	
   511	      // An empty branch never merges (Gate-2 fold G3): the writer must have
   512	      // actually committed work ahead of main.
   513	      const ahead = String((await io.execFile('git', ['log', `${trunk}..${branch}`, '--format=%H'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
   514	      if (ahead.length === 0) return park(`subtask '${st.id}' produced an EMPTY branch (no commits ahead of main) — nothing to review or merge`);
   515	
   516	      // Reviewer-seat preflight (Gate-2 fold G5): the reviewing seat must be
   517	      // listed and clean before a reviewer spawns into it.
   518	      try {
   519	        const otherBranch = String((await withSeatHealing(other, () => io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: seats[other] }))).stdout).trim();
   520	        const reviewerPre = await preflightWorktree(root, { seat: other, branch: otherBranch }, io);
   521	        if (reviewerPre.ok !== true) return park(`reviewer-seat preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${reviewerPre.refusal}`);
   522	      } catch (err) {
   523	        return park(`reviewer-seat preparation failed for subtask '${st.id}': ${String(/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err).split('\n')[0]}`);
   524	      }
   525	
   526	      // Reviewer (read-only, the OTHER seat's model and cwd, subtask-reviewer role).
   527	      const reviewPrompt = [
   528	        `You are the subtask-reviewer for subtask '${st.id}': ${st.title}.`,
   529	        `Review the change on ${branch} against ${trunk} from the ${other} seat (fresh context). Run: git diff ${trunk}..${branch} (and git log ${trunk}..${branch}) to see exactly what changed — branch refs are shared across worktrees.`,
   530	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   531	      ].join('\n\n');
   532	      // A reviewer DEATH is never a review verdict (dogfood finding D6): a
   533	      // failure-class result re-resolves the role (entry avoidance carried on
   534	      // subtaskAvoidEntries) and relaunches, bounded by the chain length —
   535	      // only a real verdict reaches the gate.
   536	      const review = await superviseVerdictChild({
   537	        roleChain: `worker-${other}`,
   538	        childRole: 'subtask-reviewer',
   539	        prompt: reviewPrompt,
   540	        seatPath: seats[other],
   541	        label: `${st.id}-review`,
   542	        subtaskId: st.id,
   543	        roleLabel: 'reviewer',
   544	      });
   545	      if (review.parked !== undefined) return review.parked;
   546	      if (review.result.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && review.result.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   547	        findings = String(review.result.findings ?? '');
   548	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   549	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED || (after.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   550	          return escalate(gate, findings, cap);
   551	        }
   552	        continue;
   553	      }
   554	
   555	      // Merger (read-only child — it re-checks; the SUPERVISOR merges).
   556	      const mergerPrompt = [
   557	        `You are the merger for subtask '${st.id}'. Adversarially re-check ${branch} against ${trunk} before it merges.`,
   558	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   559	      ].join('\n\n');
   560	      const mergerCheck = await superviseVerdictChild({
   561	        roleChain: 'merger',
   562	        childRole: 'merger',
   563	        prompt: mergerPrompt,
   564	        seatPath: root,
   565	        label: `${st.id}-merge-check`,
   566	        subtaskId: st.id,
   567	        roleLabel: 'merger',
   568	      });
   569	      if (mergerCheck.parked !== undefined) return mergerCheck.parked;
   570	      if (mergerCheck.result.verdict !== 'APPROVED' && mergerCheck.result.verdict !== 'APPROVED_WITH_NOTES') {
   571	        findings = String(mergerCheck.result.findings ?? '');
   572	        const after = await transition({ type: LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION, gate, verdict: 'BLOCKED' });
   573	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED || (after.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   574	          return escalate(gate, findings, cap);
   575	        }
   576	        continue;
   577	      }
   578	
   579	      const merge = await mergeSubtask(root, { branch, trunk }, io);
   580	      if (merge.ok !== true) return park(`merge of '${branch}' refused: ${merge.reason}`);
   581	      // The receipt lands BEFORE the phase-advance journal write (I1): a
   582	      // crash between the two resumes into a receipt-backed advance.
   583	      appendEntry(io.fs, mergesPath, { subtaskId: st.id, branch, mergedAt: io.now() });
   584	      merged = true;
   585	    }

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	// Fake `io` object matching the injected-io contract from docs/design/core.md:
     2	// { cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now }. Backed by memfs.
     3	// stdout/stderr collect writes; io.stdoutText()/io.stderrText() flatten them.
     4	// now() returns a settable ISO string (io.setNow). execFile resolves from an
     5	// execResults map keyed by the joined command string.
     6	//
     7	// Wave-B2 additions (bundle lock layer) — all optional, defaulted, and purely
     8	// additive so no prior consumer is affected:
     9	//   host / pid / startTime  — the process-identity facts the lock model writes
    10	//     into owner.json and compares on contention (see plan §Concurrency, Lock
    11	//     model iterations 3+4).
    12	//   processAlive(pid, startTime) — the "provably dead" oracle. Default: only
    13	//     THIS io's own {pid, startTime} is alive; every other descriptor reads as
    14	//     dead. Override per test to model a live foreign owner or a pid/start-time
    15	//     mismatch (reused pid).
    16	//   newFencingToken() — the injected per-acquisition token source. Deterministic
    17	//     monotonic counter ("tok-1", "tok-2", …), matching the injected-io
    18	//     discipline already used for now()/execFile so lock acquisition is
    19	//     reproducible in tests rather than drawing from Math.random.
    20	
    21	import { makeMemfs } from './memfs.mjs';
    22	
    23	export function makeIo({
    24	  files = {},
    25	  env = {},
    26	  stdin = '',
    27	  execResults = {},
    28	  now = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    29	  host = 'host-A',
    30	  pid = 4242,
    31	  startTime = 111000,
    32	  processAlive = null,
    33	  fencingSeed = 1,
    34	  execPath = process.execPath,
    35	} = {}) {
    36	  const { fs, files: filesSnapshot } = makeMemfs(files);
    37	  const outChunks = [];
    38	  const errChunks = [];
    39	  let currentNow = now;
    40	  let tokenCounter = fencingSeed - 1;
    41	  const defaultAlive = (p, s) => p === pid && s === startTime;
    42	
    43	  const stdout = { write: (s) => { outChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    44	  const stderr = { write: (s) => { errChunks.push(String(s)); return true; } };
    45	
    46	  const execFile = (cmd, args = [], /* opts */ _opts = {}) => {
    47	    const key = [cmd, ...(Array.isArray(args) ? args : [])].join(' ');
    48	    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    49	      if (!(key in execResults)) {
    50	        const e = new Error(`spawn ${cmd} ENOENT`);
    51	        e.code = 'ENOENT';
    52	        return reject(e);
    53	      }
    54	      const r = execResults[key];
    55	      const code = r.code ?? 0;
    56	      const out = r.stdout ?? '';
    57	      const err = r.stderr ?? '';
    58	      if (code !== 0) {
    59	        // util.promisify(execFile) rejects on nonzero exit; error carries code + streams.
    60	        const e = new Error(`Command failed: ${key}`);
    61	        e.code = code;
    62	        e.stdout = out;
    63	        e.stderr = err;
    64	        return reject(e);
    65	      }
    66	      resolve({ stdout: out, stderr: err });
    67	    });
    68	  };
    69	
    70	  return {
    71	    cwd: '/repo',
    72	    env,
    73	    stdin,
    74	    stdout,
    75	    stderr,
    76	    fs,
    77	    execFile,
    78	    now: () => currentNow,
    79	    setNow: (iso) => { currentNow = iso; },
    80	    execPath,
    81	    host,
    82	    pid,
    83	    startTime,
    84	    processAlive: processAlive || defaultAlive,
    85	    // Tiny retry budget: unit tests exercising live-lock refusal stay fast
    86	    // while the retry code path itself still runs (production default: 120x25ms).
    87	    lockRetry: { attempts: 2, delayMs: 2 },
    88	    newFencingToken: () => `tok-${++tokenCounter}`,
    89	    stdoutText: () => outChunks.join(''),
    90	    stderrText: () => errChunks.join(''),
    91	    files: filesSnapshot,
    92	  };
    93	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop run state — snapshot + journal + replay for the Layer-2 supervisor,
     3	 * mirroring the bundle store's crash-tolerance idioms (atomic tmp+rename
     4	 * snapshots, torn-tail-tolerant NDJSON, seq-dedup replay). Everything lives
     5	 * under <root>/.handoff/loop/; the supervisor is the sole writer. Plan:
     6	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"baton loop",
     7	 * §"Smoke gate", §"Supervisor lifetime and recovery", §"Caps".
     8	 */
     9	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
    11	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    12	
    13	export const LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA = 'baton/loop-state@1';
    14	
    15	/** The frozen status vocabulary. */
    16	export const LOOP_STATUS = Object.freeze({
    17	  RUNNING: 'running',
    18	  AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL: 'awaiting-smoke-approval',
    19	  PARKED: 'parked',
    20	  ESCALATED: 'escalated',
    21	  DONE: 'done',
    22	});
    23	
    24	/** The frozen journal event vocabulary. */
    25	export const LOOP_EVENT = Object.freeze({
    26	  PHASE_ADVANCE: 'phase-advance',
    27	  GATE_ITERATION: 'gate-iteration',
    28	  ESCALATE: 'escalate',
    29	  PARK: 'park',
    30	  RESUME: 'resume',
    31	  SMOKE_AWAIT: 'smoke-await',
    32	  SMOKE_APPROVE: 'smoke-approve',
    33	});
    34	
    35	// Every gate loops at most 5 times — a repo-wide hard invariant (AGENTS.md
    36	// §Review gates); the reducer refuses a 6th and escalates instead.
    37	const GATE_ITERATION_CAP = 5;
    38	
    39	/** @param {string} root */
    40	export function loopPaths(root) {
    41	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff/loop`;
    42	  return { dir, state: `${dir}/state.json`, journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson` };
    43	}
    44	
    45	/**
    46	 * Fresh run state — pure and deterministic from the injected io (identity
    47	 * facts + clock only; no Date.now/Math.random, which would break replay).
    48	 * @param {any} spec a VALIDATED loop spec @param {any} io
    49	 */
    50	export function initLoopState(spec, io) {
    51	  const runId = `loop-${dedupeKey({ host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime, at: io.now() }).slice(0, 12)}`;
    52	  /** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    53	  const iterations = {};
    54	  for (const p of spec.phases ?? []) {
    55	    if (/gate/.test(String(p.id))) iterations[p.id] = 0;
    56	  }
    57	  return {
    58	    schema: LOOP_STATE_SCHEMA,
    59	    runId,
    60	    goal: spec.goal,
    61	    phaseCount: Array.isArray(spec.phases) ? spec.phases.length : 0,
    62	    phaseIndex: 0,
    63	    iterations,
    64	    status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING,
    65	    parkReason: null,
    66	    escalation: null,
    67	    smokeApproval: null,
    68	    createdAt: io.now(),
    69	    journalSeq: 0,
    70	  };
    71	}
    72	
    73	/**
    74	 * The pure transition reducer. Never mutates its input; unknown events and
    75	 * refused transitions return the input state unchanged (a new reference is
    76	 * only produced for a real transition).
    77	 * @param {any} state @param {any} ev
    78	 */
    79	export function applyLoopEvent(state, ev) {
    80	  switch (ev?.type) {
    81	    case LOOP_EVENT.PHASE_ADVANCE: {
    82	      const next = state.phaseIndex + 1;
    83	      if (next >= state.phaseCount) return { ...state, phaseIndex: state.phaseCount, status: LOOP_STATUS.DONE };
    84	      return { ...state, phaseIndex: next };
    85	    }
    86	    case LOOP_EVENT.GATE_ITERATION: {
    87	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
    88	      const current = state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0;
    89	      // The 6th attempt is REFUSED: the counter stays at the cap and the run
    90	      // escalates with a record naming the gate — never a 6th run.
    91	      if (current >= GATE_ITERATION_CAP) {
    92	        return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: current } };
    93	      }
    94	      return { ...state, iterations: { ...state.iterations, [gate]: current + 1 } };
    95	    }
    96	    case LOOP_EVENT.ESCALATE: {
    97	      // Supervisors enforce spec caps LOWER than the hard invariant; this
    98	      // event persists that escalation (dogfood finding D7: a cap-3 pipeline
    99	      // exit left state.json 'running' because only the reducer's own 5-cap
   100	      // could flip the status). Idempotent — an already-escalated run keeps
   101	      // its original record.
   102	      if (state.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED) return state;
   103	      const gate = String(ev.gate);
   104	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED, escalation: { gate, iteration: state.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0 } };
   105	    }
   106	    case LOOP_EVENT.PARK:
   107	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.PARKED, parkReason: ev.reason ?? null };
   108	    case LOOP_EVENT.RESUME:
   109	      // Only a PARK is resumable; an escalation is an operator decision.
   110	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.PARKED) return state;
   111	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING, parkReason: null };
   112	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_AWAIT:
   113	      return { ...state, status: LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL };
   114	    case LOOP_EVENT.SMOKE_APPROVE: {
   115	      // Guarded transition: only meaningful while awaiting, and only with the
   116	      // caller's verifySmokeToken result stamped verified === true. A missing
   117	      // or false `verified` is unverified — it never resumes the run.
   118	      if (state.status !== LOOP_STATUS.AWAITING_SMOKE_APPROVAL) return state;
   119	      if (ev.verified !== true) return state;
   120	      // A smoke gate on the FINAL phase already advanced past phaseCount —
   121	      // approval completes the run as DONE, not RUNNING (dogfood finding D9:
   122	      // the printed done/exit 0 left a 'running' artifact behind).
   123	      const resumedStatus = state.phaseIndex >= state.phaseCount ? LOOP_STATUS.DONE : LOOP_STATUS.RUNNING;
   124	      return { ...state, status: resumedStatus, smokeApproval: { token: ev.token, at: ev.ts ?? null } };
   125	    }
   126	    default:
   127	      return state;
   128	  }
   129	}
   130	
   131	/**
   132	 * Atomic snapshot write (tmp + rename under .handoff/loop).
   133	 * @param {string} root @param {any} state @param {any} io
   134	 */
   135	export async function writeLoopState(root, state, io) {
   136	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   137	  ensureDir(io.fs, p.dir);
   138	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.state, state);
   139	}
   140	
   141	/**
   142	 * Append one journal event; allocates the next seq past
   143	 * max(snapshot.journalSeq, journal tail) unless the entry carries one.
   144	 * @param {string} root @param {any} ev @param {any} io

     1	# Goal-loop + dual-worktree pipeline (Layers 2+3, v1 plan)
     2	
     3	Status: APPROVED — Gate 1 passed at iteration 4 of 5 (2026-07-18,
     4	codex/gpt-5.5 @ xhigh, `degraded: model-fallback`).
     5	
     6	## Context
     7	
     8	baton v1 (Layer 1, cross-harness usage-limit failover) is complete: Gate-2
     9	passed, a 40-finding surface audit folded, and the live e2e handoff loop
    10	verified through the real installed plugin. Layers 2 and 3 automate the
    11	development methodology itself on top of that foundation: a goal-driven loop
    12	with review gates (Layer 2) and a dual-worktree adversarial write/review
    13	pipeline (Layer 3).
    14	
    15	## Locked decisions (Saksham, 2026-07-18)
    16	
    17	1. **Packaging**: inside the baton repo — new commands `baton loop` and
    18	   `baton pipeline`, sharing the role matrix, resolver, doctor, and
    19	   checkpoint/handoff machinery.
    20	2. **Layer 3 swap cadence**: per subtask — writer and reviewer swap seats
    21	   every subtask; the planner role plans and the merger role performs the
    22	   final adversarial merge (concrete chains live in `baton.config.json`).
    23	3. **Layer 2 smoke gate**: one staged gate after planning — the loop must
    24	   produce a minimal end-to-end slice (build + run + one real assertion) and
    25	   get explicit approval before the full run; the 5-iteration cap governs
    26	   every gate.
    27	4. **Driver**: a supervisor CLI — spawns headless role children
    28	   (`claude -p` / `codex exec`), parses verdicts, and invokes baton handoff
    29	   automatically on limit death. Survives the operator's session ending.
    30	
    31	## Design
    32	
    33	### Loop spec
    34	
    35	`loop.json` at repo root (schema `baton/loop@1`), scaffolded by
    36	`baton loop init "<goal>"`:
    37	
    38	- `goal`, `constraints[]` (falsifiable acceptance criteria — the loop's exit
    39	  condition), `smoke` ({cmd, expect} — the staged-gate slice definition),
    40	- `phases` — an ordered list of `{id, role}` entries: `id` is a free label
    41	  for progress display; `role` MUST be a matrix role ID (validation fails on
    42	  any unknown role before a child spawns). Default:
    43	  plan(`planner`) → gate-1(`plan-reviewer`) → smoke — itself a TDD mini-cycle
    44	  per the repo contract: smoke-tests(`test-author`) →
    45	  smoke-verify(`test-verifier`) → smoke-build(`implementer`) → the human
    46	  smoke gate — → per-subtask cycle (`test-author` → `test-verifier` →
    47	  `implementer` → `subtask-reviewer`) → gate-2(`final-reviewer-a`,
    48	  `final-reviewer-b`) → done,
    49	- `budgets` ({iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin, maxChildrenPerPhase}),
    50	- roles resolve through the existing `baton.config.json` matrix — never
    51	  hardcoded models.
    52	
    53	### Role-matrix additions (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 1)
    54	
    55	The loop references roles by ID only; every referenced role must exist in the
    56	matrix or `loop.json` validation fails (`unknown role '<id>'`, exit 2, before
    57	any child spawns). Four roles are ADDED to `baton.config.json` +
    58	`templates/baton.config.json.tpl` in the first implementation task:
    59	
    60	- `subtask-reviewer` — per-subtask diff review inside the loop cycle.
    61	- `merger` — the only role that writes main in pipeline mode; adversarial
    62	  re-check before merge.
    63	- `worker-a`, `worker-b` — the two pipeline seats. Subtask N assigns
    64	  writer = seat (N mod 2), reviewer = the other seat; the SEATS swap, and each
    65	  seat's concrete platform/model comes from its configured chain via the
    66	  resolver. No vendor or model name appears in this plan, child prompts, or
    67	  loop code — chains live in config only.
    68	
    69	### `baton loop` (Layer 2)
    70	
    71	- `baton loop init <goal>` — scaffold spec + plan template; idempotent,
    72	  dry-run-able, same two-phase plan/apply discipline as `baton init`.
    73	- `baton loop run [--approve-smoke <token>]` — the state machine. State lives
    74	  in gitignored `.handoff/loop/state.json` + `journal.ndjson` (same atomic
    75	  write + journal replay machinery as the bundle; loop position is also
    76	  checkpointed into the handoff bundle so a limit death carries it).
    77	
    78	### Root + ownership discipline (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 2)
    79	
    80	One authoritative root: the MAIN repo root where `loop.json` lives. The
    81	supervisor is the SOLE writer of `.handoff/loop/` and of the main root's
    82	handoff bundle; every supervisor-driven baton invocation passes explicit
    83	`--root <mainRoot>` and a supervisor-owned stable session hint
    84	(`--session loop-<runId>` — a NEW flag added to `checkpoint`; `receive`
    85	already has it), so the bundle has exactly one stable owner.
    86	
    87	**Child-hook policy (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 1; iteration 3,
    88	finding 1)** — children never touch OR read any bundle, by construction: the
    89	supervisor sets `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1` in every child's environment, and
    90	EVERY baton hook command — the hook entrypoints, `baton checkpoint`, and
    91	`baton session-start` (which would otherwise read the bundle and inject
    92	pending-handoff resume context into the child) — quietly no-ops (exit 0,
    93	empty output) when it is set. This covers
    94	Layer-2 children running in the MAIN root (where root discovery would
    95	otherwise find the supervisor-owned bundle and the foreign-session guard
    96	would reject their stable hints) and pipeline children in worktrees alike;
    97	narrative checkpointing for supervised work is the supervisor's job, driven
    98	by parsed child results. Contract tests: (a) Layer-2 — two concurrent
    99	main-root children with distinct stable session hints and live hooks →
   100	zero bundle mutations, zero foreign-session rejections, supervisor
   101	`--session` checkpoints land; (b) pipeline — same from two worktrees while
   102	the supervisor writes loop state.
   103	- Each role invocation is a headless child under the supervisor:
   104	  - claude-code roles: `claude -p "<role prompt>" --permission-mode
   105	    acceptEdits --allowedTools ...` (allowlist per role: reviewers read-only).
   106	  - codex roles: `codex exec -C <root> -s <sandbox> --model <m> -c
   107	    model_reasoning_effort=<e> "<prompt>" </dev/null` (stdin closed — the
   108	    stdin-hang class is a known field failure).
   109	  - Verdict contract: children end with the structured tail
   110	    (`VERDICT: APPROVED|APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|BLOCKED` + FINDINGS) already used
   111	    by every review gate; the supervisor parses exactly that.
   112	
   113	### Child supervision contract (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 7)
   114	
   115	`children.mjs` owns the full child lifecycle, not just argv assembly:
   116	
   117	- Spawn detached in its OWN process group; stdin closed at spawn (the
   118	  codex-exec stdin-hang class); per-role timeout → SIGTERM to the group, 10 s
   119	  grace, then SIGKILL to the group (no zombie grandchildren; reap verified in
   120	  tests with a child that forks a sleeper).
   121	- Full child output streams to `.handoff/loop/children/<child-id>.log` with a
   122	  byte cap (head preserved, middle truncated with a marker — the verdict tail
   123	  is at the END so the tail is never truncated).
   124	- **Verdict parsing is region-bounded (prompt-echo defense)**: only the
   125	  child's FINAL message region is parsed — claude-code children run with a
   126	  structured output format and the parser reads the result field; codex
   127	  children are parsed strictly AFTER the last `tokens used` marker. The
   128	  prompt region (which quotes `VERDICT:` in its instructions) is never
   129	  scanned. Multiple tails in the final region → the LAST one wins.
   130	- An unparseable, absent, or truncated verdict is BLOCKED-with-escalation,
   131	  never APPROVED; a child that exits after making edits but before a verdict
   132	  parks the subtask with its worktree preserved for inspection.
   133	- A generic nonzero exit classifies as `other-error` (existing classifier
   134	  rule) → the loop retries once, then parks; it NEVER triggers limit failover
   135	  without limit-specific evidence.
   136	- Fixture-driven tests: prompt echo, multiple verdict tails, truncated
   137	  transcript, timeout kill/reap, zombie grandchild, non-limit nonzero exit.
   138	
   139	### Supervisor lifetime and recovery (Gate-1 iteration 2, finding 5)
   140	
   141	"Survives the operator's session ending" is a defined mechanism, not a hope:
   142	
   143	- `baton loop run` is FOREGROUND by default. `--detach` re-launches the
   144	  supervisor in its own session (`setsid`), output to
   145	  `.handoff/loop/supervisor.log`, and returns.
   146	- A run lock `.handoff/loop/supervisor.lock` carries {pid, process
   147	  start-time, host, runId} — the same provably-dead machinery as the
   148	  Layer-1 bundle lock. A second `loop run` against a LIVE supervisor is
   149	  refused; against a provably-dead one it recovers.
   150	- The state machine is journal-replayable: recovery replays
   151	  `journal.ndjson`, marks any in-flight child of the dead supervisor as
   152	  interrupted (its process group is gone or killed on adoption), and either
   153	  continues from the last completed step or parks with an escalation note —
   154	  never re-runs a completed gate iteration.
   155	- Acceptance adds a supervisor-death test: kill the supervisor (and its
   156	  launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
   157	  journal and continues or parks; no orphaned children survive (process
   158	  groups reaped on adoption).
   159	
   160	- **Smoke gate (staleness-proof — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 8; TDD order —
   161	  iteration 2, finding 2)**: after gate-1, the smoke slice is built as a TDD
   162	  mini-cycle (test-author writes the failing smoke assertion → test-verifier
   163	  approves it → implementer writes only enough to pass it — the repo TDD
   164	  contract applies to the slice, not just to subtasks); the supervisor then
   165	  runs `smoke.cmd`, records output, and STOPS, writing
   166	  `.handoff/loop/SMOKE-REVIEW.md` plus `smoke-approval.json` containing an
   167	  approval token = digest over {loop-state digest, smoke.cmd, output digest,
   168	  HEAD, content-sensitive git digest (existing snapshot machinery), child
   169	  assignment}. Resuming requires `baton loop run --approve-smoke <token>`;
   170	  the supervisor recomputes the digest first and REFUSES a drifted token
   171	  (anything changed since the human looked) — it parks for a fresh smoke run
   172	  instead. No full-run budget is spent before a valid approval.
   173	- **Limit failover (the baton integration — Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 3)**:
   174	  an exact transaction, not a loose chain. On child death: (1) reap the
   175	  process group and freeze the transcript; (2) classify via `baton detect`;
   176	  non-limit → the retry/park path above. On a limit classification:
   177	  (3) checkpoint the loop position INTO THE BUNDLE (supervisor
   178	  `checkpoint --session loop-<runId>`) so the seal carries it and the receipt
   179	  token binds it; (4) seal via `finalize --reason-class usage-limit --root
   180	  <mainRoot>` (an already-dead/unsealed bundle takes the degraded-open path
   181	  receive already supports); (5) refresh probes; (6) `receive --prepare` then
   182	  `--commit` back-to-back with IDENTICAL intake flags, the supervisor session
   183	  hint, and the same explicit root, with NO intervening writes; (7) resolver
   184	  output (with the dead entry avoided) picks the relaunch platform+model;
   185	  (8) relaunch the child with the resume prompt. A stale-token rejection
   186	  triggers ONE automatic re-prepare/commit retry; a second failure parks.
   187	  Between prepare and commit the failover holds the loop transaction lock and
   188	  writes NOTHING — `.handoff/loop/` writes are not token-bound inputs, so
   189	  this is enforced by construction and ASSERTED in tests (fs spy: zero
   190	  `.handoff/loop/` writes inside the window), not assumed. Drift-rejection
   191	  tests inject the inputs the token actually binds: git change and probe
   192	  refresh between prepare and commit → stale-token → one retry path. Plus:
   193	  open-bundle (unsealed) limit death; the full seal→receive→relaunch happy
   194	  path.
   195	- **Model-level failover (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 4)**: the resolver
   196	  gains entry-level avoidance — `avoidEntries: [{platform, model}]` alongside
   197	  the existing platform-level `avoid[]` — and the classifier gains a
   198	  `model-unavailable` signature class (distinct from usage-limit; seeded with
   199	  the verified account-tier rejection string). A model rejected at spawn adds
   200	  its {platform, model} entry to avoidEntries and re-resolves: other models
   201	  on the SAME platform remain selectable. Named test: first chain entry
   202	  rejected as model-unavailable → the next entry on the same platform is
   203	  selected, with no platform-wide avoidance.
   204	  All platforms/entries exhausted → the loop parks with a resume-at hint
   205	  instead of dying.
   206	- **Caps**: every gate loops at most 5 iterations; on exhaustion the loop
   207	  parks and escalates (writes `.handoff/loop/ESCALATION.md`, notifies) — never
   208	  a 6th run.
   209	
   210	### `baton pipeline` (Layer 3 — a loop preset)
   211	
   212	- `baton pipeline run` = `loop run` with the dual-worktree subtask cycle:
   213	  - Two persistent worktrees `.worktrees/wt-a`, `.worktrees/wt-b`
   214	    (`git worktree add`); `pipeline init` ensure-lines `.worktrees/` into
   215	    `.gitignore` (same idempotent machinery as the `.handoff/` line).
   216	  - Subtask N: the writer seat implements on a namespaced branch
   217	    (`baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N`) in its worktree; the reviewer seat reviews
   218	    the diff from the OTHER worktree (fresh checkout, fresh context). Seats
   219	    swap every subtask: worker-a writes / worker-b reviews → worker-b writes /
   220	    worker-a reviews → … (concrete models per seat come from the role

   833	// H2 (A1/B2) — bounded failover: avoidEntries/avoid carry; exhaustion PARKS.
   834	describe('pipeline — H2: bounded failover (avoid carries; exhaustion parks)', () => {
   835	  // A recording runner that emits a platform-appropriate death banner and THROWS
   836	  // once a bound is exceeded, so an unbounded ping-pong terminates the test RED.
   837	  function boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound, banner }) {
   838	    const calls = [];
   839	    const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts) => {
   840	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args: (spec?.args ?? []).map(String) });
   841	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} child spawns (ping-pong)`);
   842	      const text = typeof banner === 'function' ? banner(spec) : banner;
   843	      io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
   844	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, `working...\n${text}\n`);
   845	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: 1, verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   846	    };
   847	    fn.calls = calls;
   848	    return fn;
   849	  }
   850	  const modelsOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => valAfter(c.args, '-s') === 'workspace-write').map((c) => valAfter(c.args, '--model'));
   851	
   852	  it('RED (H2a): always-model-unavailable on a two-entry codex chain never re-picks a rejected model; all rejected -> PARK (bounded)', async () => {
   853	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['codex/wa1@xhigh', 'codex/wa2@xhigh'] } };
   854	    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), config, runner: undefined });
   855	    const runner = boundedDeathRunner(io, { bound: 3, banner: MODEL_UNAVAIL }); // correct impl parks at 2 writers
   856	    io.superviseChild = runner;
   857	    io.__runner = runner;
   858	    let code = 'ran';
   859	    try { code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io); } catch { code = 'unbounded'; }
   860	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'both models rejected -> the subtask parks (bounded, no infinite ping-pong)');
   861	    // BOTH entries are attempted, in order, exactly once — then it parks.
   862	    assert.deepEqual(modelsOf(runner), ['wa1', 'wa2'], `each chain entry is tried once, in order; got ${JSON.stringify(modelsOf(runner))}`);
   863	    assert.ok(runner.calls.length <= 3, `the runner stayed within the throwing bound (no ping-pong); calls=${runner.calls.length}`);
   864	  });
   865	
   866	  it('RED (H2b): always-usage-limit across a cross-platform chain parks within a bounded child budget (no infinite loop)', async () => {
   867	    const config = { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG, roles: { ...PIPELINE_CONFIG.roles, 'worker-a': ['claude-code/wa-cc', 'codex/wa-cx@xhigh'] } };

   877	    let code = 'ran';

   879	    assert.equal(code, 4, 'a repeatedly-limit-dying subtask parks within the child budget, never loops forever');

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md | sed -n '206,340p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
   206	- **Caps**: every gate loops at most 5 iterations; on exhaustion the loop
   207	  parks and escalates (writes `.handoff/loop/ESCALATION.md`, notifies) — never
   208	  a 6th run.
   209	
   210	### `baton pipeline` (Layer 3 — a loop preset)
   211	
   212	- `baton pipeline run` = `loop run` with the dual-worktree subtask cycle:
   213	  - Two persistent worktrees `.worktrees/wt-a`, `.worktrees/wt-b`
   214	    (`git worktree add`); `pipeline init` ensure-lines `.worktrees/` into
   215	    `.gitignore` (same idempotent machinery as the `.handoff/` line).
   216	  - Subtask N: the writer seat implements on a namespaced branch
   217	    (`baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N`) in its worktree; the reviewer seat reviews
   218	    the diff from the OTHER worktree (fresh checkout, fresh context). Seats
   219	    swap every subtask: worker-a writes / worker-b reviews → worker-b writes /
   220	    worker-a reviews → … (concrete models per seat come from the role
   221	    matrix — see Role-matrix additions).
   222	  - Verdict flow: APPROVED → the merger role adversarially re-checks and
   223	    merges into main (the ONLY writer to main); BLOCKED → writer fixes,
   224	    ≤5 iterations, then escalate. After each merge both worktrees
   225	    fast-forward.
   226	
   227	  **Worktree transaction layer (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 5)** —
   228	  `worktrees.mjs` treats every git operation as a guarded transaction:
   229	  - Branch namespace `baton/wt-*/…` is loop-owned; anything else is never
   230	    touched.
   231	  - Preflight before each child: worktree exists in `git worktree list`,
   232	    index clean, HEAD on the expected branch — a dirty or drifted worktree
   233	    parks the subtask instead of spawning.
   234	  - Postflight after each child: HEAD still on the expected branch, main
   235	    unmoved by the child — violation parks with the evidence.
   236	  - Merges run under a supervisor-held merge lock; worktree sync after merge
   237	    is ff-only (a non-ff sync is a corruption signal → park); merge conflicts
   238	    park + escalate, never auto-resolved.
   239	  - Stale metadata self-heals: a raw-deleted worktree directory is detected
   240	    (`git worktree list` vs fs), pruned (`git worktree prune`), and re-added;
   241	    teardown uses `git worktree remove` + branch deletion so acceptance
   242	    constraint 5 (no residue) holds.
   243	  - Adversarial tests: stale branch from a previous run, dirty worker,
   244	    main moved mid-subtask, merge conflict, raw-deleted `.worktrees/`,
   245	    untracked `.worktrees/` (missing gitignore line → doctor check).
   246	
   247	  **Attribution enforcement for child commits (Gate-1 iteration 1,
   248	  finding 6)** — the invariant is enforced, not assumed:
   249	  - The supervisor sets `GIT_AUTHOR_NAME/EMAIL` and `GIT_COMMITTER_NAME/EMAIL`
   250	    (sole author identity) in EVERY child's environment.
   251	  - Every merge is gated on an attribution scan over the exact range
   252	    `main..baton/wt-<seat>/subtask-N` — author/committer identity plus the
   253	    forbidden-trailer patterns (the existing doctor patterns, applied to the
   254	    full range, not a last-50 window). A violation blocks the merge and
   255	    parks.
   256	  - The existing doctor 50-commit scan remains the repo-wide backstop.
   257	
   258	### Module layout (all under `core/src/loop/`)
   259	
   260	`spec.mjs` (load/validate loop.json incl. role-existence check) · `state.mjs`
   261	(state machine + journal + smoke-approval token) · `children.mjs` (full child
   262	lifecycle: spawn/process-group/timeout/log-cap, region-bounded verdict parse,
   263	detect-on-exit) · `failover.mjs` (the limit-death transaction) ·
   264	`worktrees.mjs` (guarded git transactions, branch discipline, attribution
   265	range scan) · `commands/loop.mjs`, `commands/pipeline.mjs`. Plus three Layer-1
   266	extensions: `roles/resolve.mjs` gains `avoidEntries[]` (entry-level
   267	avoidance, backward-compatible — omitted means current behavior); the
   268	signature table gains the `model-unavailable` class; `checkpoint` gains a
   269	`--session <hint>` flag (supervisor identity), and the
   270	`BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD` no-op guard lands in the hook entrypoints,
   271	`checkpoint`, AND `session-start` (every hook-invoked command).
   272	
   273	### Acceptance constraints (the loop's exit condition)
   274	
   275	1. On a fixture repo, `baton loop run` drives a toy goal end-to-end headlessly:
   276	   plan → gate-1 → smoke slice AS A TDD MINI-CYCLE (failing smoke assertion
   277	   authored and verifier-approved BEFORE the slice implementation — order
   278	   asserted from the journal) → parked for approval → (approved) → 2 subtasks
   279	   TDD → gate-2 → done, with review artifacts under `reviews/` in the fixture.
   280	   Layer-2 children run in the main root with live hooks and neither mutate
   281	   NOR read the supervisor-owned bundle: under `BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD=1`,
   282	   hook commands on all three platforms produce zero bundle writes AND zero
   283	   session-start resume context (both asserted).
   284	2. Simulated limit death (child exits with a verbatim limit banner) mid-subtask
   285	   → the loop seals, remaps the role off the dead platform, resumes on the
   286	   other harness, and completes the subtask. Chained A→B→A tested. Separately:
   287	   a model-unavailable rejection at spawn selects the NEXT entry on the same
   288	   platform (no platform-wide avoidance).
   289	3. `baton pipeline run` completes 2 subtasks with alternating writer/reviewer
   290	   across the two worktrees; the attribution range scan passes for main AND
   291	   both worker branch ranges (child commits included) — sole author, zero AI
   292	   attribution; reviewer children run read-only.
   293	4. The 5-cap parks + escalates on every gate (unit-forced exhaustion test);
   294	   no gate ever runs a 6th iteration. Killing the supervisor (and its
   295	   launching session) mid-subtask → a fresh `loop run` recovers via the
   296	   journal and continues or parks, with no orphaned children.
   297	5. Deleting `.handoff/` and `.worktrees/` leaves the fixture repo exactly as
   298	   a plain git repo (no residue outside gitignored trees).
   299	6. All existing 878 tests stay green; the new suite covers spec validation,
   300	   state-machine transitions (unit, injected io), child verdict parsing
   301	   (fixture transcripts), failover (fake children), and worktree discipline.
   302	
   303	### Top risks
   304	
   305	1. **Headless permission surfaces** — Claude Code children need permission
   306	   flags that stay inside the sandboxed repo; reviewers get read-only
   307	   allowlists. Mitigation: per-role allowlist table + spike task 0.
   308	2. **Verdict-parse drift** — models sometimes wrap the tail; the
   309	   region-bounded parser (Child supervision contract) takes the LAST tail in
   310	   the final-message region only and treats an unparseable one as
   311	   BLOCKED-with-escalation, never as APPROVED.
   312	3. **Cost runaway** — per-phase child caps + iteration caps + the smoke gate
   313	   before any full-run spend; every child's token/duration logged to the loop
   314	   journal.
   315	4. **Worktree state divergence** — only the merger writes main; workers are
   316	   branch-jailed; ff-sync after each merge; `baton pipeline doctor` checks
   317	   worktree health.
   318	5. **Cross-platform child API drift** — the spawn contract lives in ONE module
   319	   (children.mjs) with per-platform fixtures, same seam discipline as
   320	   normalize.mjs.
   321	
   322	## Deferred from v1 of this milestone (Gate-2 fold, recorded 2026-07-19)
   323	
   324	Explicitly deferred — recorded so the shipped surface is never read as
   325	implementing them (Gate-2 findings G8–G13): `loop run --detach` (the
   326	supervisor is foreground-only in v1; recovery via the run lock + journal is
   327	implemented and tested); probe-cache integration in the loop/pipeline
   328	resolvers (probes: null — resolution is degraded-flagged offline behavior);
   329	a smoke gate in pipeline mode (the smoke gate is a loop-mode surface);
   330	findings persistence across supervisor restarts (findings are in-memory per
   331	invocation; the journal carries gate iterations, not findings text);
   332	reviews/ artifact generation from loop children; per-phase (vs global)
   333	maxChildrenPerPhase accounting; a live-transcript codex marker fixture;
   334	streaming log caps + head/tail classification buffers; child-log secret
   335	redaction + inclusion in the purge walk; deriving the trunk branch instead
   336	of assuming 'main'. These are v1.1 hardening items unless a gate re-raises
   337	one as blocking.
   338	
   339	## Out of scope for this milestone
   340

     1	// In-memory fs fake for unit tests. Synchronous subset only.
     2	// Atomicity contract: renameSync moves content in a single step, so a target
     3	// path is never observed carrying partial content. Every mutation appends a
     4	// full snapshot to `fs.__history`, letting tests enumerate intermediate states
     5	// and prove the tmp+rename discipline (target's first appearance is a rename).
     6	
     7	function fsError(code, syscall, path) {
     8	  const e = new Error(`${code}: ${syscall} '${path}'`);
     9	  e.code = code;
    10	  e.syscall = syscall;
    11	  e.path = path;
    12	  return e;
    13	}
    14	
    15	function normalize(p) {
    16	  const str = String(p);
    17	  const isAbs = str.startsWith('/');
    18	  const out = [];
    19	  for (const part of str.split('/')) {
    20	    if (part === '' || part === '.') continue;
    21	    if (part === '..') {
    22	      out.pop();
    23	      continue;
    24	    }
    25	    out.push(part);
    26	  }
    27	  return (isAbs ? '/' : '') + out.join('/');
    28	}
    29	
    30	function dirname(np) {
    31	  const i = np.lastIndexOf('/');
    32	  if (i <= 0) return '/';
    33	  return np.slice(0, i);
    34	}
    35	
    36	function basename(np, parent) {
    37	  return parent === '/' ? np.slice(1) : np.slice(parent.length + 1);
    38	}
    39	
    40	export function makeMemfs(initialFiles = {}) {
    41	  const store = new Map(); // path -> string content
    42	  const dirs = new Set(['/']);
    43	  const mtimes = new Map();
    44	  const history = [];
    45	  let clock = 0;
    46	
    47	  const snapshot = () => Object.fromEntries(store);
    48	  const record = (op, paths) => history.push({ op, paths, files: snapshot() });
    49	  const touch = (np) => mtimes.set(np, ++clock);
    50	
    51	  const mkdirp = (np) => {
    52	    let cur = '';
    53	    for (const part of np.split('/').filter(Boolean)) {
    54	      cur = cur + '/' + part;
    55	      dirs.add(cur);
    56	    }
    57	  };
    58	
    59	  const ensureParent = (np, syscall) => {
    60	    const parent = dirname(np);
    61	    if (parent === '/' ) return;
    62	    if (!dirs.has(parent)) throw fsError('ENOENT', syscall, np);
    63	  };
    64	
    65	  for (const [p, content] of Object.entries(initialFiles)) {
    66	    const np = normalize(p);
    67	    mkdirp(dirname(np));
    68	    store.set(np, String(content));
    69	    touch(np);
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  const fs = {
    73	    readFileSync(p /*, enc */) {
    74	      const np = normalize(p);
    75	      if (!store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOENT', 'open', np);
    76	      return store.get(np);
    77	    },
    78	
    79	    writeFileSync(p, data /*, enc */) {
    80	      const np = normalize(p);
    81	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
    82	      store.set(np, String(data));
    83	      touch(np);
    84	      record('writeFileSync', [np]);
    85	    },
    86	
    87	    appendFileSync(p, data) {
    88	      const np = normalize(p);
    89	      ensureParent(np, 'open');
    90	      store.set(np, (store.get(np) || '') + String(data));
    91	      touch(np);
    92	      record('appendFileSync', [np]);
    93	    },
    94	
    95	    renameSync(from, to) {
    96	      const nf = normalize(from);
    97	      const nt = normalize(to);
    98	      if (store.has(nf)) {
    99	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
   100	        const content = store.get(nf);
   101	        store.delete(nf);
   102	        store.set(nt, content);
   103	        touch(nt);
   104	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
   105	        return;
   106	      }
   107	      // Directory rename (atomic move of the node and all descendants) — the
   108	      // lock's dead-reclaim arbitration renames the stale lock dir aside.
   109	      if (dirs.has(nf)) {
   110	        ensureParent(nt, 'rename');
   111	        const pre = nf + '/';
   112	        dirs.delete(nf);
   113	        dirs.add(nt);
   114	        for (const k of [...store.keys()]) {
   115	          if (k.startsWith(pre)) {
   116	            store.set(nt + k.slice(nf.length), store.get(k));
   117	            store.delete(k);
   118	          }
   119	        }
   120	        for (const d of [...dirs]) {
   121	          if (d !== nt && d.startsWith(pre)) {
   122	            dirs.add(nt + d.slice(nf.length));
   123	            dirs.delete(d);
   124	          }
   125	        }
   126	        touch(nt);
   127	        record('renameSync', [nf, nt]);
   128	        return;
   129	      }
   130	      throw fsError('ENOENT', 'rename', nf);
   131	    },
   132	
   133	    mkdirSync(p, opts = {}) {
   134	      const np = normalize(p);
   135	      if (opts.recursive) {
   136	        mkdirp(np);
   137	        record('mkdirSync', [np]);
   138	        return undefined;
   139	      }

   152	    readdirSync(p) {
   153	      const np = normalize(p);
   154	      if (!dirs.has(np)) {
   155	        if (store.has(np)) throw fsError('ENOTDIR', 'scandir', np);

   161	      };
   162	      for (const k of store.keys()) collect(k);
   163	      for (const d of dirs) collect(d);
   164	      return [...names];
   165	    },

   476	  const P = loopPaths('/repo');
   477	  const findingsLines = (io, gate) => {
   478	    const raw = io.files()[`${P.dir}/findings/${gate}.ndjson`];
   479	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
   480	  };
   481	  const seedRunningState = (io, over) => {
   482	    io.fs.mkdirSync(P.dir, { recursive: true });
   483	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/state.json`, JSON.stringify({
   484	      schema: 'baton/loop-state@1', runId: 'loop-item4', goal: 'Ship the loop', phaseCount: 1, phaseIndex: 0,
   485	      iterations: {}, status: 'running', parkReason: null, escalation: null, smokeApproval: null, createdAt: T0, journalSeq: 0,
   486	      flavor: 'loop', ...over,
   487	    }, null, 2) + '\n');
   488	  };
   489	
   490	  it('RED (4a): a BLOCKED gate then PARK then resume carries the persisted findings into the next child prompt', async () => {
   491	    const marker = 'DISTINCTIVE_FINDINGS_4A_MARKER';
   492	    const cfg = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(CONFIG));
   493	    cfg.roles['solo-codex'] = ['codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh']; // single entry — a model death parks
   494	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'solo-codex' }] });
   495	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, files: { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(cfg, null, 2), '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(ownedBundle('codex', 'open'), null, 2) + '\n' }, runner: undefined });
   496	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   497	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 0, findings: marker }, // records the gate findings
   498	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: `child died: ${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n` }, // model death → single-entry chain parks
   499	    ]);
   500	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   501	    assert.equal(await cmdLoop(['run'], io), 4, 'run 1 parks after recording the BLOCKED findings');
   502	    assert.ok(findingsLines(io, 'gate-1').some((l) => String(l.findings).includes(marker)), 'the BLOCKED findings were persisted to findings/gate-1.ndjson');
   503	
   504	    const runner2 = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   505	    io.superviseChild = runner2;
   506	    io.__runner = runner2;
   507	    await cmdLoop(['resume'], io);
   508	    assert.ok(runner2.calls.length >= 1, 'resume re-spawned the gate child');
   509	    assert.match((runner2.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the resumed child prompt embeds the persisted prior findings');
   510	  });
   511	
   512	  it('RED (4b loop): a re-invoke with status running + a persisted findings line embeds it in the FIRST child prompt', async () => {
   513	    const marker = 'PERSISTED_FINDINGS_4B_LOOP';
   514	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }] });
   515	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   516	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]);
   517	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   518	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } });
   519	    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
   520	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/gate-1.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: marker, at: T0 }) + '\n');
   521	
   522	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   523	    assert.ok(io.__runner.calls.length >= 1, 'a child spawned on re-invoke');
   524	    assert.match((io.__runner.calls[0].args ?? []).map(String).join(' '), new RegExp(marker), 'the first re-invoke child prompt embeds the persisted findings');
   525	  });
   526	
   527	  it('RED (4c loop): a fresh-invoke cap escalation embeds the PERSISTED findings in ESCALATION.md', async () => {
   528	    const marker = 'PERSISTED_FINDINGS_4C_LOOP';
   529	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 1, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
   530	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   531	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [{ verdict: 'APPROVED' }]); // unused — escalates before spawn
   532	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   533	    seedRunningState(io, { specDigest: dedupeKey(spec.phases), iterations: { 'gate-1': 1 } }); // AT cap 1
   534	    io.fs.mkdirSync(`${P.dir}/findings`, { recursive: true });
   535	    io.fs.writeFileSync(`${P.dir}/findings/gate-1.ndjson`, JSON.stringify({ iteration: 1, findings: marker, at: T0 }) + '\n');
   536	
   537	    const code = await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   538	    assert.equal(code, 3, 'a gate at the cap escalates');
   539	    assert.match(loopFile(io, 'ESCALATION.md') ?? '', new RegExp(marker), 'ESCALATION.md embeds the persisted findings even when in-memory findings are empty');
   540	  });
   541	
   542	  it('RED (4d): the findings file is append-only across iterations (two BLOCKED verdicts → two ordered lines)', async () => {
   543	    const spec = loopSpec({ phases: [{ id: 'gate-1', role: 'plan-reviewer' }], budgets: { iterationCap: 5, perRoleTimeoutMin: 30, maxChildrenPerPhase: 10 } });
   544	    const io = makeLoopRepo({ spec, runner: undefined });
   545	    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, [
   546	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'first-finding-A' },
   547	      { verdict: 'BLOCKED', findings: 'second-finding-B' },
   548	      { verdict: 'APPROVED' },
   549	    ]);
   550	    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
   551	    await cmdLoop(['run'], io);
   552	    const lines = findingsLines(io, 'gate-1');
   553	    assert.equal(lines.length, 2, 'two BLOCKED verdicts append two findings lines (append-only, never overwrite)');
   554	    assert.match(String(lines[0].findings), /first-finding-A/, 'the first BLOCKED findings is line 1');
   555	    assert.match(String(lines[1].findings), /second-finding-B/, 'the second BLOCKED findings is line 2 (order preserved)');
   556	  });

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Append-only JSONL journal IO, tolerant of the corruption modes a
     3	 * kill-mid-write can produce: a torn (unterminated) tail, a corrupt middle
     4	 * line, and out-of-order sequence numbers.
     5	 */
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * Append one entry as a single JSON line.
     9	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    10	 */
    11	export function appendEntry(fs, path, obj) {
    12	  fs.appendFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(obj) + '\n');
    13	}
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * Read every parseable entry. Never throws.
    17	 * Warnings: {line, kind: 'torn-tail'|'parse-error'|'seq-order', ...}.
    18	 * A non-monotonic numeric seq is flagged but the entry is kept — replay and
    19	 * audit must still see it.
    20	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    21	 * @returns {{entries: any[], warnings: any[]}}
    22	 */
    23	export function readAllTolerant(fs, path) {
    24	  /** @type {any[]} */
    25	  const entries = [];
    26	  /** @type {any[]} */
    27	  const warnings = [];
    28	
    29	  let raw;
    30	  try {
    31	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    32	  } catch {
    33	    return { entries, warnings };
    34	  }
    35	  if (raw === '') return { entries, warnings };
    36	
    37	  const terminated = raw.endsWith('\n');
    38	  const lines = raw.split('\n');
    39	  if (terminated) lines.pop();
    40	
    41	  let prevSeq = null;
    42	  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i += 1) {
    43	    const lineNo = i + 1;
    44	    const isLast = i === lines.length - 1;
    45	    const torn = isLast && !terminated;
    46	    let parsed;
    47	    try {
    48	      parsed = JSON.parse(lines[i]);
    49	    } catch {
    50	      warnings.push(torn ? { line: lineNo, kind: 'torn-tail' } : { line: lineNo, kind: 'parse-error' });
    51	      continue;
    52	    }
    53	    if (typeof parsed?.seq === 'number') {
    54	      if (prevSeq !== null && parsed.seq <= prevSeq) {
    55	        warnings.push({ line: lineNo, kind: 'seq-order', prev: prevSeq, seq: parsed.seq });
    56	      }
    57	      prevSeq = prevSeq === null ? parsed.seq : Math.max(prevSeq, parsed.seq);
    58	    }
    59	    entries.push(parsed);
    60	  }
    61	  return { entries, warnings };
    62	}

A wrong implementation could include the marker while still appending the D8 fallback footer, whose current branch is defined at [pipeline.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/core/src/commands/pipeline.mjs:225) and guarded separately at [pipeline-run.test.mjs](/Users/saksham/baton/tests/commands/pipeline-run.test.mjs:1194).
